Scope: accepted shared-candidate-evidence-packet §3.1a's actual collector changes, generator,
original source outcomes and once-only material hand-off to Nudge. Separate read-only reviewer;
no reviewer code edits, staging or commits.

Decision: **no blocking defect in this bounded implementation**. This is not coordinated consumer
cutover acceptance, semantic qualification, full module completion or a full-gate pass.

Reviewed actual five source routes, complete retained operands, terminal-fork negatives, original
exposure-unavailable object identity, once-only exposure collection per event/wide row, narrowing,
readings-only private prerequisites and unchanged whole-selection incomplete-denominator refusal.
Nudge retains the original collected material@2 evidence without a second invocation.
Independent normal-Make verification passes133 cases across six files.

Still open: actual Hint predecessor fork/promotion dispatch and current matrix/factory/compiler/
module/wire/seat cutover; Threat availability; remaining current consumers; new independently
grounded successor semantic cases/populations; full gates. The eight failed canonical predecessor
cases are not repaired by this review.

Accepted semantic-validation-authority §§3.3/4.1/5.1/7.2/8 Slice B give the next path:

1. Add distinct new exact-@2 case IDs and independent proposition bindings. The current case
   version grammar remains literal1; do not invent case version2 or a retirement state.
2. The ten cited material cases may reuse pinned Python-chess text/meaningful expectations only
   with new exact-@2 proposition/case records and recomputed ordinary authority digests.
3. The three frozen existing-assertion cases cannot change subject or refresh their frozen hashes.
   Those hashes bind subject, operation, input and expectation. Use fresh independent authority
   for exposure and the additional material assertion, not the old receipt.
4. Execute the current operations, point successor profile arms at those new refs and regenerate
   normally. Re-run the complete population; old census receipts cannot qualify successors.
5. Preserve predecessor failures/debt during partial migration. Old negative@1 absence when only
   @2 is emitted is not useful current qualification.
6. After actual D3851 caller retirement, remove retired subjects' cases/profile refs from the
   live registry atomically, retaining historical meanings, tests and dated receipts. Do not
   invent a compiler1 validation operation to make the instrument green.

Reviewer: `objective_source_review`; read-only contract/symbol audit, no implementation or
self-acceptance. **No existing accepted representation closes this seam.** The D3802
text-only/conditional geometry rule in evidence-presentation2269–2276 is specifically for the
six `derived.inspector.detail.*@1` envelopes, not every whole source topic. The implementation
correctly limits that rule to Full Inspector2 × those six exact refs
(`presentation-contract.ts:1371–1397`). It cannot be widened by reusing the detail condition
on another projection or by a caller choosing fewer composition members.

Actual local collection (`inspector-local-source.ts:48–63`) retains a genuine singleton whole
reading even when its internal arrays/flagged subset are empty. The dependent owner likewise
distinguishes actual zero values from nonempty values (`inspector-dependent-source.ts:104–108`).
The source-order clause in module-registration2461–2478 requires every original occurrence
and exact owned whole bundle; it does not authorize dropping an admitted reading because its
legacy presentation has nothing drawable. The same RFC's closed outcomes3442–3455 supply only
topic-level no_witness, Explorer-only below_floor, or available facts with presented/deferred.
None carries a truthful new non-budget occurrence status implicitly. A nonempty original
collection cannot be relabelled no_witness while quietly losing its ordinal, nor can a missing
drawable component be called budget-deferred with no exceeded dimension.

There are at least three different operand cases, not one universal “empty array” case:

- Existing zero-capable summaries (e.g. pawn contacts, space, pawn connectivity, legal-move
  counts) can have meaningful literal zeros but their square/relation composition refuses.
  Pawn contacts with no attack edge can still retain passed-pawn/connected-passer data; absence
  of a drawable contact does not establish absence of every observation in that source.
- Nonempty original readings may yield no flagged display subset (`loose_piece`) or contain
  explicit per-color unavailable data (destinations/control). Some current templates require
  min-one arrays or index the first witness (threat/mate), so calling them first and catching
  their exception is not an admitted absence renderer.
- A positive back-rank reading can have a heavy piece already on fileTarget
  (`presentation-inspector-adapters.ts:768–777`). The relation helper correctly removes
  from==to and then refuses zero edges (450–456). That does not make the positive source empty;
  a zero-length move arrow or “no back-rank evidence” substitute would be false.

Generic pending/abstention components do not solve occurrence retention: they belong to an
asked question/operation with `evidenceRef:null`, not the original source value; the current
receipt parser admits that seat only under its exact registered question projection. Rendering
one of six detail projections inside a source fact also changes the projection/disclosure
identity; detail is an explicit separate door, not a fallback alias. Raw structured documents,
weaker source substitution and silently narrowed consumer forms are not accepted escapes.

**Minimal amendment obligations, in the existing coordinated module/presentation RFCs:**

1. Enumerate the exact affected whole-source refs and their closed, source-backed cases:
   true empty population, nonempty reading with zero selected flags, non-drawable positive
   geometry and actual unavailable/mixed operand states. Preserve all original values and
   source ordinals; do not copy the test-only witness predicate into a new source issuer or
   change factory predicates/output. Explicitly retain the actual zero-value collection path.
2. Choose the per-occurrence presentation contract. The least structural change is a genuine
   source-owned registered statement, with optional board members only under exact authored
   whole-reading cases. Reuse existing honest-zero parsers/templates where sufficient; author
   closed retained operands/fixed labels where min-one/first-witness templates cannot represent
   the source. Back-rank self-target means already occupying, not an invented movement edge or
   lost positive claim. No global empty-square/relation parser waiver, all-clear/move-grade
   inference or change to other consumers is justified.
3. Author exact Full2 pair/composition/form coverage and source-retention assertions for those
   cases. Potential forms still cover the binding; actual text/marks derive from the same
   admitted complete value, with correct conventions/availability, mandatory owned caption,
   complete component membership and no arbitrary predicate callback. This need not create a
   new evidence projection/version merely to render unchanged source operands; any changed
   source meaning would require its own explicit version rather than silent reuse.
4. Retain original collection/source keys, ordinal multiplicity and the source projection in
   page/disclosure and component receipts. A genuine text-only source fact can stay presented
   within the existing available outcome and actual atomic word/fact budget. If an author
   instead chooses a new non-fact status/disposition, its exact wire/disclosure/parser/renderer,
   original occurrence identity/counting and non-budget meaning must be specified and reviewed;
   it is not already supplied by no_witness/deferred/below_floor. Never fabricate budget cost.
   Detail remains a separately requested exact view, including valid zero-member detail pages.
5. Require genuine full source→adapter→compiler→wire/receiver controls for zero counts, populated
   but unflagged pieces, passed pawns without contact edges, unavailable/mixed per-color data,
   nonempty self-target back rank and ordinary positives. Keep original ordinal/range/source
   identity, actual budgets and siblings; reject copied/crossed/missing/extra components,
   false no-witness/deferred, fabricated empty geometry and detail-as-source receipt swaps.
   Source/current permission/disclosure/cancellation checks still precede publication. Preserve
   old consumer behavior rather than broadening legacy renderer grammars implicitly.

This is an authoring gap under an already recorded defect, not a new owner-policy question or
permission to implement those choices before independent amendment acceptance. Other inactive
request/inventory primitives may proceed under their existing authority. No new test run or
full-gate result is asserted by this contract-only audit; the preceding376-case/typecheck
receipts remain the bounded current implementation proof, not a source-page clearance.

   directions. A genuine `rules.phase.reading@2` row can instead carry another non-objective

   fires. Its registered MAP adapter (`presentation-inspector-adapters.ts:1107`) still has no
   wire hook; only the Full2 source rows receive the hook at1130/1145. ReviewMapScreen:401 renders
   those parsed items. Preserve the four existing D3947 objective negatives while extending
   exact semantic validation to the real remaining MAP pairs; do not label that bounded repair
   generic completion.

3. **Story's actual wire receiver does not pin presentation ownership.**
   `story.ts:396–412` requires one title fact and nonempty moment components, but not
   review.story@1 nor the exact derived.story.title@2 title pair/renderer. A valid foreign fact
   receipt can replace the title or a moment without a Story-envelope digest check binding that

   parser does not enforce the same contract. DrillApi.story calls this parser atapi.ts:1527.
   Current App.fetchStory uses that call for coordinator side effects and renders Review Map,
   not these Story receipt components; this is therefore a reachable API admission defect, not

   same-consumer wrong-title-pair controls, then check exact title and moment consumer ownership

4. **The Corpus receiver pins renderer but not binding/convention.**
   `inspector-evidence.ts:48–68` validates consumer/source, renderer, floor and population fields.
   Its `consumer.explorer_population@1` fact can retain identical operands and text while
   changing binding to recorded_run or convention to another registered convention: the generic
   fact parser accepts either closed vocabulary independently (`presentation-contract.ts:914–920`),
   and Corpus adds no required declared_convention/explorer-population@1 check. The actual
   DrillScreen Corpus panel renders corpusPresentation at3144. Modern human-split already
   enforces its exact binding/convention at human-split-response.ts:76, so retain that stronger
   control rather than copying its gap elsewhere.

5. **Declared producer association is also missing outside Full2.**
   The generic receipt parser joins evidenceRef.projection to adapter.projection but only parses
   evidenceRef.producer as a versioned name. Modern human-split and Corpus do not compare it to
   the projection's compiled producer either. Full2's hook already performs the literal manifest
   producer join (`presentation-inspector-adapters.ts:102–103`). A changed producer with fully
   recomputed public digests must fail at other exact receivers too. Derive that check from the
   existing compiled projection declaration; do not invent source authority from its digest.

contract beside each real constructor (including nested captions and exact binding/convention),

### D3948 remaining adapter closure — independent implementation-shape recommendation

Read-only review of the actual play/consumer/Inspector builders identifies a bounded refactor,
not a need for another renderer registry. A private builder in the existing presentation kit
can capture the literal renderer/binding/convention once and return both typed construction
and parsed-operand validation. The adapter/family captures that same object; construction uses
its statement/operand method, while validateWireComponent checks either the top-level
fact_statement operand or nested square_set.caption. No adapter permissions are learned by
executing its constructor against a sample, the first observed payload, or a collected set of
outputs. Existing FACT_OPERAND_PARSERS remain responsible for operand grammar/text recomputation.

Concrete implementation seams and their cost are structural rather than new feature scope:

- `presentation-consumer-adapters.ts` already routes most facts through factAdapter, which
  receives the literal tuple once. Generate its constructor and hook together there. For
  structuralCaption, capture the convention from the declared loop kind rather than letting
  an arriving observation choose it, and retain the source-kind join. This is the cheapest
  common-helper conversion, not a per-consumer renderer whitelist.
- `presentation-inspector-adapters.ts` Family/add currently has no hook to retain/forward.
  Add that seam and use bound-fact helpers for single statements, squaresLine captions and
  relationList statements. The existing transition helper hides renderer selection inside
  build(event)'s returned id; move that literal id to transition's declaration argument and
  let its callback return operands only. All twelve existing transition rows already supply
  literal renderers, so no discovery mechanism is needed.
- structuralEvent is shared by eight declared source families; make its family/kind a
  declaration parameter so construction and validation share the convention and expected
  observation kind. Similarly parameterize pivotal's four source kinds, preserving the
  human-divergence recorded_run distinction instead of accepting every convention in a union.
- Play's local add/statement helpers need the same family contract propagated alongside
  construction. Its structural/named-structure, square captions, relation+statement and
  literal family/rung loops can use that shared builder. Guided-hint tuple checks do not replace
  the existing separate delivery parser or its ceiling/context checks.
- Genuine Compare2 namedStructure/non-named variants need two literal bound contracts beside
  the existing branch. The production selector chooses those objects; the receiving adapter
  permits those exact tuples, not any globally valid renderer. The client cannot infer which
  private source branch actually ran from a re-digested wire item, and this refactor must not
  claim otherwise. Existing public discriminants can be checked where retained; no invented
  private source authority or payload reconstruction is required.

Existing Full2 whole readings and six details have stronger state/geometry/source-context
checks. Do not replace those hooks with tuple-only validation. Any later deduplication must
retain zero/populated/not_evaluated variants, conditional geometry, source-owner association
and exact complete compositions in their existing owner. Likewise retain non-fact magnitude,
move-path, citation and relation semantics rather than attaching a wildcard successful hook.

After the real fact/caption builders are converted, registration can refuse an adapter whose
static component/composition includes fact_statement or square_set but supplies no wire hook.
This prevents future omissions without a second list of covered pairs. Falsifiers must keep a
genuine adapter and source ref, substitute another valid renderer/binding/convention (including
nested captions), and recompute every public digest; all existing genuine variants and complete
compositions must still pass. This is a recommended implementation shape under existing exact
adapter authority, not acceptance of unbuilt code or a claim that D3948 is closed. No production,
tracker, schema, public wire or permissions changes and no test execution occur in this review.

### D4058 overload/deflection coexistence — independent source-scope read

Main reports actual selected REST delivery improving from zero to16 of18 after the accepted
comparator insertion. Both remaining overload cells retain genuine overload and deflection
descriptions; existing relative order selects deflection first and the combined complete text
exceeds the unchanged50-word budget. This is not proof of a missing overload source, permission
to truncate a caption or evidence that the word ceiling should rise.

Actual overloadExploitationObservedOperands requires a three-capture path, one original defender
with at least two duties, its recapture of the first capturer, and a positive capture of the
other original duty target. Crucially, it does not require the defender's second duty to be absent
after relocation. It also does not call the separate recorded-target-survival filter; this read
does not infer a survival counterexample from that absence, since the legal capture geometry
also constrains which original pieces can move or disappear. In contrast,
deflectionObservedOperands explicitly requires that lost duty after the reply, and its current
recordedDeflectionObservedOperands wrapper additionally requires original-target survival. Thus
overload@3 does not generally entail deflection@3; adding a broad directed subsumption would
overstate the actual collector predicate.

For simultaneously admitted witnesses, exact anchors/FENs/UCIs, original and relocated defender,
lost-duty target identity and identical final exchange could establish a bounded overlap. Those
are differently shaped fields, however: current SUBSUMPTION only compares equal-named retained
fields and permits rules entailment, not arbitrary family-based duplicate suppression. A new
normalization/overlap rule would need its own precise contract and negatives for another window,
defender, target or exchange; neither co-occurrence nor shared prose is sufficient. It would also
need to state what source meaning the surviving caption actually preserves.

The smaller honest remedy is a declared relative product-convention order placing overload
before deflection within the already finite eight-family Nudge group, not claiming either is
strategically better or deleting evidence. This requires a bounded independently reviewed
revision of D4058's current promise to preserve existing within-group ties before implementation.
Critical-first, exactness/lift/ties outside that precise relation, non-Nudge bytes, other admitted
facts, source semantics and fixed budgets must remain. No tests/heavy jobs, production/RFC edits
or semantic/native/release clearance by this reviewer; only this existing receipt is appended.

The proposed whole-position pawn_file_group snapshot {fen,groups[]} and file_state snapshot
{fen,files[8]} are sound source-authority shapes, but need a narrow cardinality clarification
before their factories: current §3.0 explicitly lists only six complete snapshots, whereas

existing clause/table with these two exact sources, not new ids or a population registry.

domain and original input receipt. Its two color island lists already enumerate every pawn.
Occupied (color,file) group rows retain all §3.3 operands, while no pawns yields available
groups[]. The file snapshot always contains each a–h file exactly once, both color pawn sets
and one of the four states; zero pawns means eight open entries, not missing evidence.
Before/after events consume genuine ordered snapshots plus the exact legal move, including
empty→empty, without treating absent positive rows as negative authority.

The clarification should also fix file_state event equality/emission. Advancing a pawn changes
its retained square while leaving the open/half-open/closed enum unchanged; the present wording
does not explicitly distinguish that from a file-class event. Pin the intended rule instead of
letting factory shape select it accidentally. Rank-only island pawn motion likewise cannot be
mislabelled topology_changed merely because retained member squares differ: accepted §3.6
explicitly says the same file partition emits nothing. Original exact members remain payload
operands regardless of the selected equality key.

destination is eligible afterward. It does not check the destination's previous class; the
catalogue's already-classified wording must not be silently strengthened into that extra test.
New file_access_revealed should require the same color/role heavy occupant at the same square
before/after, excluding a relocated/captured/newly promoted piece, and retain the original
genuine file_state event/cause. Mutual exclusion is per physical piece/edge/cause, not an
edge-wide suppression that discards other stationary rooks/queens.

The literal becomes boundary also needs pinning: stationary own-half-open→open is a new open
state, while open→own-half-open must not be called newly revealed access. A neutral bounded
rule consistent with the accepted wording is after=open with before not open, or after the
piece's own half-open with before neither open nor its own half-open. This retains newly open
access without losing half-open→open or inventing a positive open→half-open event. State both
classes and cause; infer no activity, control or improvement. This is a recommended precise
